Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # JADE external archive and success-history memory\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\n        archive_size = max(pop_size, 10)\n        memory_len = 5\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_len, float(self.F))\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_len, float(self.CR))\n        memory_pos = 0\n        p_best = float(self.params.get('p_best', 0.2))\n\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # JADE success-history memory parameters\n            # Controller-provided F/CR act as initial memory defaults and exploration bias\n            F_ctrl = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_ctrl = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            p_best = float(params.get('p_best', p_best))\n\n            # Optional: use controller values to bias this generation's memory sampling\n            gen_memory_F = np.concatenate([memory_F, np.array([F_ctrl])])\n            gen_memory_CR = np.concatenate([memory_CR, np.array([CR_ctrl])])\n\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest mutation)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE (current-to-pbest/1)\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Cauchy/randn mutation factors per individual\n            F_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n            CR_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_best_p - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr =         # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'view', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py'},\n        \"tool_output\": \"Here's the result of running `cat -n` on /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py:\n     1\t\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n     2\t\n     3\tProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\n     4\tThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\n     5\tDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        #   it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n        else:\n            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\n            return\n\n        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n        transition = (\n            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n            self._last_action_idx,\n            reward,\n            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n            done\n        )\n\n        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n        else:\n            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n\n        self.step_count += 1\n\n        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest mutation)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE (current-to-pbest/1)\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Cauchy/randn mutation factors per individual\n            F_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n            CR_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_best_p - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr = np.array(SCR, dtype=float)\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(np.sum(weights * SCR_arr), 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_len\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

JADE external archive and success-history memory\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\n        archive_size = max(pop_size, 10)\n        memory_len = 5\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_len, float(self.F))\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_len, float(self.CR))\n        memory_pos = 0\n        p_best = float(self.params.get('p_best', 0.2))\n\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # JADE success-history memory parameters\n            # Controller-provided F/CR act as initial memory defaults and exploration bias\n            F_ctrl = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_ctrl = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            p_best = float(params.get('p_best', p_best))\n\n            # Optional: use controller values to bias this generation's memory sampling\n            gen_memory_F = np.concatenate([memory_F, np.array([F_ctrl])])\n            gen_memory_CR = np.concatenate([memory_CR, np.array([CR_ctrl])])\n\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest mutation)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE (current-to-pbest/1)\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Cauchy/randn mutation factors per individual\n            F_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n            CR_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_best_p - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr = np.array(SCR, dtype=float)\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(np.sum(weights * SCR_arr), 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_len\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"

JADE success-history memory parameters\n            # Controller-provided F/CR act as initial memory defaults and exploration bias\n            F_ctrl = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_ctrl = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            p_best = float(params.get('p_best', p_best))\n\n            # Optional: use controller values to bias this generation's memory sampling\n            gen_memory_F = np.concatenate([memory_F, np.array([F_ctrl])])\n            gen_memory_CR = np.concatenate([memory_CR, np.array([CR_ctrl])])\n\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sorted indices for p-best selection (pbest mutation)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE (current-to-pbest/1)\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Cauchy/randn mutation factors per individual\n            F_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n            CR_i_list = np.empty(pop_size, dtype=float)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution around a random memory entry\n                attempts = 0\n                F_i = F_ctrl\n                while attempts < 20:\n                    m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_F.shape[0]))\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + gen_memory_F[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                    if F_i > 0.0:\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                F_i_list[i] = F_i\n\n                # Sample CR_i from normal distribution around a random memory entry\n                m_idx = int(rng.randint(gen_memory_CR.shape[0]))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.randn() * 0.1 + gen_memory_CR[m_idx], 0.0, 1.0))\n                CR_i_list[i] = CR_i\n\n                # Choose p-best from top p*pop_size individuals\n                p_num = max(1, int(round(p_best * pop_size)))\n                p_idx = int(sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, p_num)])\n                x_best_p = pop[p_idx]\n\n                # Select two distinct random indices from pop and archive\n                # Use union of pop + archive for greater diversity\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if candidates:\n                    r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n                else:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n\n                # Choose r2 from union(pop, archive), cannot equal i or r1\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0:\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n                        x_r2 = archive[r2]\n                    else:\n                        r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_best_p - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = pop[i].copy()\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip to bounds\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors with JADE selection and archive updates\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                # Greedy selection with archive\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old position before replacing\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        # Replace a random archive member\n                        replace_pos = int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))\n                        archive[replace_pos] = pop[i]\n\n                    # Track successful parameter values\n                    SF.append(F_i_list[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_i_list[i])\n                    S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE success-history memory\n            if len(SF) > 0:\n                deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                weights = deltas / (np.sum(deltas) + 1e-12)\n\n                # Lehmer mean for F\n                SF_arr = np.array(SF, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr * SF_arr))\n                denom_F = float(np.sum(weights * SF_arr))\n                if denom_F > 1e-12:\n                    memory_F[memory_pos] = min(1.0, max(0.0, sum_F / denom_F))\n\n                # Weighted mean for CR (standard mean)\n                SCR_arr = np.array(SCR, dtype=float)\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(np.sum(weights * SCR_arr), 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_len\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Designed for use with\\n    the L-SHADE style linear population size reduction in ec_algorithm.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 p_best_start=0.3, p_best_end=0.1, stagnation_boost=0.25,\\n                 stagnation_threshold=6):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.p_best_start = p_best_start\\n        self.p_best_end = p_best_end\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.stagnation_threshold = stagnation_threshold\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta):\\n        self.at = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation):\\n        self.at = observation.get('iteration', self.at + 1)\\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', 10000))\\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\\n\\n        F = self.f_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.f_end * progress\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.cr_end * progress\\n        p_best = self.p_best_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.p_best_end * progress\\n\\n        gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\\n        if gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = gbest\\n\\n        if self.stagnation_count >= self.stagnation_threshold:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            p_best = max(0.05, p_best + 0.1)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'p_best': float(p_best),\\n                'strategy': 'jade'}\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",
